327	                  processing={processingOrderId === order.id}
328	                  onApprove={() => handleApprove(order.id)}
329	                  onDecline={() => handleDecline(order.id)}
330	                />
331	              ))
332	            )}
333	          </div>
334	        </div>
335	
336	        {/* Demand chart */}
337	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4">
338	          <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">
339	            {tx.localDemand}
340	          </h2>
341	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5 mb-4">
342	            {tx.localDemandHelp}
343	          </p>
344	
345	          {demandBars.length === 0 ? (
346	            <div className="text-center py-6">
347	              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">{tx.noDemandSignals}</p>
348	              <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs mt-1">{tx.shareProfileLink}</p>
349	            </div>
350	          ) : (
351	            <div className="space-y-3">
352	              {demandBars.map((bar) => {
353	                const pct = Math.round((bar.total_qty / demandBars[0].total_qty) * 100)
354	                return (
355	                  <div key={bar.crop_name}>
356	                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
357	                      <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">{bar.crop_name}</span>
358	                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-medium">{bar.total_qty} kg</span>
359	                    </div>
360	                    <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
361	                      <div
362	                        className="h-full bg-green-600 rounded-full"
363	                        style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
364	                      />
365	                    </div>
366	                  </div>
367	                )
368	              })}
369	            </div>
370	          )}
371	        </div>
372	
373	        {/* Add listing button */}
374	        {profileComplete ? (
375	          !showForm && (
376	            <button
377	              onClick={() => setShowForm(true)}
378	              className="w-full bg-white border-2 border-green-700 text-green-700 font-bold py-4 rounded-2xl text-base flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:bg-green-50"
379	            >
380	              <span className="text-xl leading-none">+</span>
381	              {tx.addNewProduce}
382	            </button>
383	          )
384	        ) : (
385	          <div className="w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 font-semibold py-4 rounded-2xl text-sm text-center">
386	            {tx.completeBeforeAdd}
387	          </div>
388	        )}
389	
390	        {/* Listing form */}
391	        {showForm && profileComplete && (
392	          <ProduceListingForm
393	            farmerId={farmer!.id}
394	            farmerSlug={farmer!.slug}
395	            defaultMethod={farmer!.method}
396	            onClose={() => setShowForm(false)}
397	            onPublished={() => { setShowForm(false); loadDashboard() }}
398	          />
399	        )}
400	      </div>
401	
402	      {/* Manage listings modal */}
403	      {showListings && farmer && (
404	        <ManageListingsModal
405	          farmerId={farmer.id}
406	          defaultMethod={farmer.method ?? 'natural'}
407	          onClose={() => setShowListings(false)}
408	          onChanged={loadDashboard}
409	        />
410	      )}
411	
412	      {/* Edit profile modal */}
413	      {showProfileEdit && farmer && (
414	        <ProfileEditModal
415	          farmer={farmer}
416	          onClose={() => {
417	            // If profile still incomplete, don't allow close (keep banner as fallback)
418	            if (isProfileComplete(farmer)) setShowProfileEdit(false)
419	            else setShowProfileEdit(false) // allow dismiss; banner still shown
420	          }}
421	          onSaved={(updated) => {
422	            setFarmer(updated)
423	            setShowProfileEdit(false)
424	          }}
425	        />
426	      )}
427	    </main>
428	  )
429	}
430	
431	/* ─── Profile edit modal ─────────────────────────────────── */

433	  farmer,

435	  onSaved,
436	}: {
437	  farmer: Farmer
438	  onClose: () => void

440	}) {

446	  const [sinceYear, setSinceYear] = useState(
447	    farmer.farming_since_year ? String(farmer.farming_since_year) : '',
448	  )

582	  },
583	}
584	
585	export type TranslationKey = keyof typeof t.en
586	
587	export function bilingual(key: TranslationKey): string {
588	  const en = (t.en as Record<string, string>)[key] ?? ''
589	  const te = (t.te as Record<string, string>)[key] ?? ''
590	  if (!te || en === te) return en
591	  // Long / multi-line text (e.g. WhatsApp templates, descriptions) → stack with separator.
592	  if (en.length > 60 || en.includes('\n') || te.includes('\n')) {
593	    return `${en}\n\n---\n\n${te}`
594	  }
595	  return `${en} / ${te}`
596	}
597

1493	    const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60)
1494	    if (hrs < 24) return `${hrs}h ago`
1495	    return `${Math.floor(hrs / 24)}d ago`
1496	  }

OTP table for secure farmer login\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS farmer_otps (\n  id          uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  phone       text        NOT NULL,\n  otp         text        NOT NULL,\n  expires_at  timestamptz NOT NULL,\n  used        boolean     DEFAULT false,\n  created_at  timestamptz DEFAULT now()\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_phone   ON farmer_otps(phone);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_expires ON farmer_otps(expires_at);\n\n-- RLS: only service-role key (used in API routes) can access\nALTER TABLE farmer_otps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n"

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/auth/send-otp/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}))\n  const rawPhone: string = body.phone ?? ''\n  const digits = rawPhone.replace(/\\D/g, '').slice(-10)\n\n  if (digits.length !== 10) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Enter a valid 10-digit phone number.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n\n  const accountSid = process.env.TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID\n  const authToken  = process.env.TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN\n  const fromNumber = process.env.TWILIO_PHONE_NUMBER\n\n  if (!accountSid || !authToken || !fromNumber) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: 'SMS service is not configured. Contact support.' },\n      { status: 503 },\n    )\n  }\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  // Invalidate any existing unused OTPs for this phone\n  await supabase\n    .from('farmer_otps')\n    .update({ used: true })\n    .eq('phone', digits)\n    .eq('used', false)\n\n  // Generate a 6-digit OTP, store with 10-minute expiry\n  const otp = String(Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000))\n  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString()\n\n  const { error: insertErr } = await supabase\n    .from('farmer_otps')\n    .insert({ phone: digits, otp, expires_at: expiresAt })\n\n  if (insertErr) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: insertErr.message }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n\n  // Send via Twilio SMS REST API (no npm package needed)\n  const msgBody =\n    `Your YourFamilyFarmer login code: ${otp}\\n` +\n    `Valid for 10 minutes.\\n` +\n    `Do not share this code with anyone.`\n\n  const twilioRes = await fetch(\n    `https://api.twilio.com/2010-04-01/Accounts/${accountSid}/Messages.json`,\n    {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',\n        Authorization: `Basic ${Buffer.from(`${accountSid}:${authToken}`).toString('base64')}`,\n      },\n      body: new URLSearchParams({\n        To:   `+91${digits}`,\n        From: fromNumber,\n        Body: msgBody,\n      }),\n    },\n  )\n\n  if (!twilioRes.ok) {\n    const errBody = await twilioRes.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: errBody.message ?? 'Failed to send OTP. Please try again.' },\n      { status: 502 },\n    )\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true })\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/auth/verify-otp/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}))\n  const rawPhone: string = body.phone ?? ''\n  const otp: string = String(body.otp ?? '').trim()\n  const digits = rawPhone.replace(/\\D/g, '').slice(-10)\n\n  if (digits.length !== 10 || !otp) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Phone and OTP are required.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  // Look up a valid (unused, not expired) OTP for this phone\n  const { data: record } = await supabase\n    .from('farmer_otps')\n    .select('id')\n    .eq('phone', digits)\n    .eq('otp', otp)\n    .eq('used', false)\n    .gt('expires_at', new Date().toISOString())\n    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })\n    .limit(1)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!record) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: 'Invalid or expired OTP. Please try again.' },\n      { status: 401 },\n    )\n  }\n\n  // Mark OTP as used immediately so it can't be reused\n  await supabase.from('farmer_otps').update({ used: true }).eq('id', record.id)\n\n  // Look up farmer by any common phone format stored in DB\n  const { data: farmers } = await supabase\n    .from('farmers')\n    .select('id, name, slug, phone')\n    .or(\n      [\n        `phone.eq.${digits}`,\n        `phone.eq.0${digits}`,\n        `phone.eq.+91${digits}`,\n        `phone.eq.91${digits}`,\n      ].join(','),\n    )\n    .limit(1)\n\n  let farmer = farmers?.[0]\n\n  if (farmer) {\n    // Make sure existing farmer is active\n    await supabase.from('farmers').update({ active: true }).eq('id', farmer.id)\n  } else {\n    // Auto-create a minimal farmer record on first login\n    const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)\n    const slug = `f-${digits}-${rand}`\n    const { data: created, error: insertErr } = await supabase\n      .from('farmers')\n      .insert({\n        phone: digits,\n        slug,\n        name: '',\n        village: '',\n        district: '',\n        method: 'natural',\n        region_slug: 'tadepalligudem',\n        active: true,\n      })\n      .select('id, name, slug, phone')\n      .single()\n\n    if (insertErr || !created) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not create farmer profile.' }, { status: 500 })\n    }\n    farmer = created\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, farmerId: farmer.id, farmerSlug: farmer.slug })\n}\n"
}

  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport { useState } from 'react'\nimport { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'\nimport Link from 'next/link'\nimport LanguageToggle from '@/components/LanguageToggle'\nimport { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'\n\ntype Step = 'phone' | 'otp'\n\nexport default function FarmerLoginPage() {\n  const router = useRouter()\n  const { tx } = useLang()\n  const [step, setStep]     = useState<Step>('phone')\n  const [phone, setPhone]   = useState('')\n  const [otp, setOtp]       = useState('')\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)\n  const [error, setError]   = useState('')\n\n  const digits = phone.replace(/\\D/g, '').slice(-10)\n  const canSend = digits.length === 10\n\n  const handleSendOtp = async () => {\n    if (!canSend) return\n    setLoading(true)\n    setError('')\n\n    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/send-otp', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ phone: digits }),\n    })\n    const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    setLoading(false)\n\n    if (!res.ok) {\n      setError(json.error ?? 'Could not send OTP. Please try again.')\n      return\n    }\n    setOtp('')\n    setStep('otp')\n  }\n\n  const handleVerify = async () => {\n    if (!otp.trim()) return\n    setLoading(true)\n    setError('')\n\n    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/verify-otp', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ phone: digits, otp: otp.trim() }),\n    })\n    const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    setLoading(false)\n\n    if (!res.ok) {\n      setError(json.error ?? tx.otpInvalid)\n      return\n    }\n\n    localStorage.setItem('yff_farmer_id', json.farmerId)\n    localStorage.setItem('yff_farmer_slug', json.farmerSlug)\n    router.replace('/farmer/dashboard')\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-4\">\n      <div className=\"absolute top-4 right-4\">\n        <LanguageToggle />\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"w-full max-w-sm\">\n        {/* Logo */}\n        <div className=\"text-center mb-8\">\n          <Link href=\"/consumer\" className=\"inline-flex flex-col items-center\">\n            <div className=\"w-16 h-16 bg-green-700 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-3\">\n              <span className=\"text-white font-black text-lg\">YFF</span>\n            </div>\n          </Link>\n          <h1 className=\"text-2xl font-extrabold text-gray-900\">\n            Farmer Login / రైతు లాగిన్\n          </h1>\n          <p className=\"text-gray-500 text-sm mt-1\">\n            {step === 'phone'\n              ? 'Sign in with WhatsApp / వాట్సాప్ తో సైన్ ఇన్'\n              : tx.otpSentMsg.replace('{phone}', digits)}\n          </p>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 p-6 space-y-4\">\n          {/* ── Step 1: Phone number ── */}\n          {step === 'phone' && (\n            <>\n              <div>\n                <label className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 block mb-2\">\n                  WhatsApp Number / వాట్సాప్ నంబర్\n                </label>\n                <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n                  <span className=\"flex items-center px-3 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-gray-600 font-medium whitespace-nowrap\">\n                    +91\n                  </span>\n                  <input\n                    type=\"tel\"\n                    inputMode=\"numeric\"\n                    placeholder=\"9876543210\"\n                    value={phone}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}\n                    onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleSendOtp()}\n                    className=\"flex-1 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none\"\n                    maxLength={10}\n                  />\n                </div>\n              </div>\n\n              {error && (\n                <p className=\"text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2\">{error}</p>\n              )}\n\n              <button\n                onClick={handleSendOtp}\n                disabled={loading || !canSend}\n                className=\"w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base disabled:opacity-50 active:bg-green-800 transition-colors\"\n              >\n                {loading ? tx.sending : tx.sendOtp}\n              </button>\n\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 text-center leading-snug\">\n                New here? Just enter your number and continue — we&apos;ll set up your profile.<br />\n                కొత్తవారా? మీ నంబర్ నమోదు చేసి కొనసాగండి.\n              </p>\n            </>\n          )}\n\n          {/* ── Step 2: OTP entry ── */}\n          {step === 'otp' && (\n            <>\n              <div>\n                <label className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 block mb-2\">\n                  {tx.enterOtp} / కోడ్ నమోదు చేయండి\n                </label>\n                <input\n                  type=\"tel\"\n                  inputMode=\"numeric\"\n                  placeholder={tx.otpPlaceholder}\n                  value={otp}\n                  onChange={(e) => setOtp(e.target.value.replace(/\\D/g, '').slice(0, 6))}\n                  onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleVerify()}\n                  className=\"w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-base text-center tracking-widest font-bold focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none\"\n                  autoFocus\n                  maxLength={6}\n                />\n                <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1 text-center\">\n                  Sent to +91 {digits} · Valid 10 minutes\n                </p>\n              </div>\n\n              {error && (\n                <p className=\"text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2\">{error}</p>\n              )}\n\n              <button\n                onClick={handleVerify}\n                disabled={loading || otp.length < 6}\n                className=\"w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base disabled:opacity-50 active:bg-green-800 transition-colors\"\n              >\n                {loading ? tx.verifying : tx.verifyOtp}\n              </button>\n\n              <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between text-xs\">\n                <button\n                  onClick={() => { setStep('phone'); setOtp(''); setError('') }}\n                  className=\"text-gray-500 underline\"\n                >\n                  {tx.changeNumber}\n                </button>\n                <button\n                  onClick={handleSendOtp}\n                  disabled={loading}\n                  className=\"text-green-700 font-semibold underline disabled:opacity-50\"\n                >\n                  {tx.resendOtp}\n                </button>\n              </div>\n            </>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        <p className=\"text-center text-xs text-gray-400 mt-6\">\n          <Link href=\"/consumer\" className=\"text-green-700 underline\">\n            Browse produce instead / పంట బ్రౌజ్ చేయండి\n          </Link>\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    </main>\n  )\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/farmer/dashboard/page.tsx",
  "old_string": "    const [listingsRes, pendingRes, approvedRes, intentsRes] = await Promise.all([\n      supabase.from('produce_listings').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'available'),\n      supabase.from('orders').select('*').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'pending').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),\n      supabase.from('orders').select('id, total_price').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'approved').gte('created_at', new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString()),\n      supabase.from('demand_intents').select('crop_name, quantity_kg').eq('region_slug', farmerData.region_slug).eq('fulfilled', false),\n    ])\n\n    setActiveListings(listingsRes.count ?? 0)\n    setPendingOrders((pendingRes.data ?? []) as Order[])\n    const approved = approvedRes.data ?? []\n    setApprovedCount(approved.length)\n    setTotalRevenue(approved.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0))",
  "new_string": "    const monthStart = new Date()\n    monthStart.setDate(1)\n    monthStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)\n\n    const [listingsRes, pendingRes, approvedRes, intentsRes, monthlyRes] = await Promise.all([\n      supabase.from('produce_listings').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'available'),\n      supabase.from('orders').select('*').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'pending').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),\n      supabase.from('orders').select('id, total_price').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'approved').gte('created_at', new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString()),\n      supabase.from('demand_intents').select('crop_name, quantity_kg').eq('region_slug', farmerData.region_slug).eq('fulfilled', false),\n      supabase.from('orders').select('id, total_price, created_at').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'approved').gte('created_at', monthStart.toISOString()),\n    ])\n\n    setActiveListings(listingsRes.count ?? 0)\n    setPendingOrders((pendingRes.data ?? []) as Order[])\n    const approved = approvedRes.data ?? []\n    setApprovedCount(approved.length)\n    setTotalRevenue(approved.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0))\n\n    // Monthly earnings\n    const monthly = monthlyRes.data ?? []\n    setMonthlyRevenue(monthly.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0))\n    setMonthlyOrderCount(monthly.length)\n\n    // Break into 4 weekly buckets (days 1-7, 8-14, 15-21, 22+)\n    const weeks = [0, 0, 0, 0]\n    for (const o of monthly) {\n      const day = new Date(o.created_at).getDate()\n      const bucket = day <= 7 ? 0 : day <= 14 ? 1 : day <= 21 ? 2 : 3\n      weeks[bucket] += o.total_price ?? 0\n    }\n    setWeeklyEarnings(weeks)"
}

'natural')\n  const [sinceYear, setSinceYear] = useState(\n    farmer.farming_since_year ? String(farmer.farming_since_year) : '',\n  )\n  const [pickupLocations, setPickupLocations] = useState<string[]>(\n    Array.isArray(farmer.pickup_locations) ? farmer.pickup_locations : [],\n  )\n  const [newPickup, setNewPickup] = useState('')\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)\n  const [error, setError] = useState('')",
  "new_string": "  const { tx } = useLang()\n  const [name, setName] = useState(farmer.name ?? '')\n  const [village, setVillage] = useState(farmer.village ?? '')\n  const [district, setDistrict] = useState(farmer.district ?? '')\n  const [method, setMethod] = useState(farmer.method ?? 'natural')\n  const [sinceYear, setSinceYear] = useState(\n    farmer.farming_since_year ? String(farmer.farming_since_year) : '',\n  )\n  const [pickupLocations, setPickupLocations] = useState<string[]>(\n    Array.isArray(farmer.pickup_locations) ? farmer.pickup_locations : [],\n  )\n  const [newPickup, setNewPickup] = useState('')\n\n  // Cover photo\n  const [coverFile, setCoverFile] = useState<File | null>(null)\n  const [coverPreview, setCoverPreview] = useState('')\n  const [existingCoverUrl, setExistingCoverUrl] = useState(farmer.cover_photo_url ?? '')\n\n  // Avatar photo\n  const [avatarFile, setAvatarFile] = useState<File | null>(null)\n  const [avatarPreview, setAvatarPreview] = useState('')\n  const [existingAvatarUrl, setExistingAvatarUrl] = useState(farmer.photo_url ?? '')\n\n  // Pesticide cert\n  const [certFile, setCertFile] = useState<File | null>(null)\n  const [certPreview, setCertPreview] = useState('')\n  const [existingCertUrl, setExistingCertUrl] = useState(farmer.pesticide_cert_url ?? '')\n\n  // Pickup schedule\n  const ALL_DAYS = ['Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday', 'Sunday']\n  const [slotDays, setSlotDays] = useState<string[]>(farmer.pickup_slots?.days ?? [])\n  const [slotFrom, setSlotFrom] = useState(farmer.pickup_slots?.time_from ?? '08:00')\n  const [slotTo, setSlotTo]   = useState(farmer.pickup_slots?.time_to   ?? '12:00')\n\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)\n  const [error, setError] = useState('')\n\n  const handlePickFile = (\n    e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,\n    setFile: (f: File | null) => void,\n    setPreview: (s: string) => void,\n    currentPreview: string,\n  ) => {\n    const file = e.target.files?.[0]\n    if (!file) return\n    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) { setError(tx.pickImageFile); return }\n    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError(tx.imageTooLarge); return }\n    setError('')\n    if (currentPreview) URL.revokeObjectURL(currentPreview)\n    setFile(file)\n    setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(file))\n  }\n\n  const uploadProfileImage = async (file: File, pathSuffix: string): Promise<string | null> => {\n    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'\n    const path = `${farmer.id}/${pathSuffix}.${ext}`\n    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage\n      .from('farm-images')\n      .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: true })\n    if (upErr) { setError(`Upload failed: ${upErr.message}`); return null }\n    const { data } = supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path)\n    return data.publicUrl\n  }\n\n  const toggleDay = (day: string) => {\n    setSlotDays((prev) =>\n      prev.includes(day) ? prev.filter((d) => d !== day) : [...prev, day],\n    )\n  }"
}

  "new_string": "  const handleSave = async () => {\n    if (!name.trim()) { setError(tx.nameRequired); return }\n    if (!village.trim()) { setError(tx.villageRequired); return }\n    setLoading(true)\n    setError('')\n\n    // Build unique slug from name if current slug is still auto-generated\n    const isAutoSlug = /^f-\\d{10}-[a-z0-9]{4}$/.test(farmer.slug ?? '')\n    const baseSlug = name\n      .toLowerCase()\n      .trim()\n      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')\n      .replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '')\n      .slice(0, 40) || 'farmer'\n    let newSlug = farmer.slug\n    if (isAutoSlug && baseSlug) {\n      const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5)\n      newSlug = `${baseSlug}-${rand}`\n    }\n\n    // Upload photos in parallel\n    const [newCoverUrl, newAvatarUrl, newCertUrl] = await Promise.all([\n      coverFile ? uploadProfileImage(coverFile, 'cover') : Promise.resolve(null),\n      avatarFile ? uploadProfileImage(avatarFile, 'avatar') : Promise.resolve(null),\n      certFile  ? uploadProfileImage(certFile,  'pesticide-cert') : Promise.resolve(null),\n    ])\n    if (error) { setLoading(false); return } // uploadProfileImage sets error on failure\n\n    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {\n      name:             name.trim(),\n      village:          village.trim(),\n      district:         district.trim(),\n      method,\n      slug:             newSlug,\n      pickup_locations: pickupLocations,\n      cover_photo_url:  newCoverUrl ?? existingCoverUrl || null,\n      photo_url:        newAvatarUrl ?? existingAvatarUrl || null,\n      pesticide_cert_url: newCertUrl ?? existingCertUrl || null,\n      pickup_slots: slotDays.length > 0\n        ? { days: slotDays, time_from: slotFrom, time_to: slotTo }\n        : null,\n    }\n    if (sinceYear) payload.farming_since_year = Number(sinceYear)\n\n    const { data, error: err } = await supabase\n      .from('farmers')\n      .update(payload)\n      .eq('id', farmer.id)\n      .select('*')\n      .single()\n\n    setLoading(false)\n\n    if (err || !data) {\n      setError(err?.message ?? tx.couldNotSave)\n      return\n    }\n\n    localStorage.setItem('yff_farmer_slug', data.slug)\n    onSaved(data)\n  }"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/consumer/orders/page.tsx",
  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport { useState } from 'react'\nimport Link from 'next/link'\nimport { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'\nimport { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'\nimport LanguageToggle from '@/components/LanguageToggle'\n\ntype Order = {\n  id: string\n  produce_name: string | null\n  quantity: number | null\n  unit: string | null\n  total_price: number | null\n  pickup_location: string | null\n  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'declined'\n  created_at: string\n  farmer?: {\n    name: string\n    slug: string\n    village: string\n  } | null\n}\n\nexport default function ConsumerOrdersPage() {\n  const { tx } = useLang()\n  const [phone, setPhone]     = useState('')\n  const [orders, setOrders]   = useState<Order[]>([])\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)\n  const [searched, setSearched] = useState(false)\n  const [error, setError]     = useState('')\n\n  const digits = phone.replace(/\\D/g, '').slice(-10)\n  const canSearch = digits.length === 10\n\n  const handleSearch = async () => {\n    if (!canSearch) return\n    setLoading(true)\n    setError('')\n    setSearched(false)\n\n    const { data, error: err } = await supabase\n      .from('orders')\n      .select('id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, pickup_location, status, created_at, farmer_id')\n      .eq('buyer_phone', digits)\n      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })\n      .limit(50)\n\n    if (err) {\n      setError(err.message)\n      setLoading(false)\n      return\n    }\n\n    // Fetch farmer names in one query\n    const farmerIds = [...new Set((data ?? []).map((o) => o.farmer_id).filter(Boolean))]\n    let farmerMap: Record<string, { name: string; slug: string; village: string }> = {}\n\n    if (farmerIds.length > 0) {\n      const { data: farmers } = await supabase\n        .from('farmers')\n        .select('id, name, slug, village')\n        .in('id', farmerIds)\n      farmerMap = Object.fromEntries(\n        (farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, { name: f.name, slug: f.slug, village: f.village }]),\n      )\n    }\n\n    const result: Order[] = (data ?? []).map((o) => ({\n      ...o,\n      farmer: farmerMap[o.farmer_id] ?? null,\n    }))\n\n    setOrders(result)\n    setSearched(true)\n    setLoading(false)\n  }\n\n  const statusColor = (s: string) =>\n    s === 'approved'\n      ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'\n      : s === 'declined'\n      ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'\n      : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'\n\n  const statusLabel = (s: string) =>\n    s === 'approved' ? '✓ Confirmed' : s === 'declined' ? '✕ Declined' : '⏳ Pending'\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16\">\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div className=\"bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-4\">\n          <Link href=\"/consumer\" className=\"text-green-300 text-sm flex items-center gap-1\">\n            ← Back / వెనక్కు\n          </Link>\n          <LanguageToggle />\n        </div>\n        <h1 className=\"text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight\">{tx.myOrders}</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-green-400 text-sm mt-1\">\n          Your complete order history / మీ ఆర్డర్ల చరిత్ర\n        </p>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4 max-w-lg mx-auto\">\n        {/* Phone lookup card */}\n        <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-3\">\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-600 leading-snug\">\n            {tx.enterPhoneForOrders}\n          </p>\n          <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n            <span className=\"flex items-center px-3 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-gray-600 font-medium whitespace-nowrap\">\n              +91\n            </span>\n            <input\n              type=\"tel\"\n              inputMode=\"numeric\"\n              placeholder=\"9876543210\"\n              value={phone}\n              onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}\n              onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleSearch()}\n              className=\"flex-1 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none\"\n              maxLength={10}\n            />\n          </div>\n          {error && (\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2\">{error}</p>\n          )}\n          <button\n            onClick={handleSearch}\n            disabled={loading || !canSearch}\n            className=\"w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base disabled:opacity-50 active:bg-green-800\"\n          >\n            {loading ? 'Searching... / వెతుకుతోంది' : tx.lookupOrders}\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Results */}\n        {searched && orders.length === 0 && (\n          <div className=\"text-center py-14\">\n            <div className=\"text-5xl mb-3\">📭</div>\n            <p className=\"font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm\">{tx.noOrdersYet}</p>\n            <Link href=\"/consumer\" className=\"mt-4 inline-block text-green-700 text-sm underline font-semibold\">\n              Browse produce → / పంట చూడండి\n            </Link>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {orders.length > 0 && (\n          <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n            <p className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide\">\n              {orders.length} order{orders.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} found\n            </p>\n            {orders.map((order) => (\n              <div key={order.id} className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden\">\n                <div className=\"p-4 space-y-2\">\n                  <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-2\">\n                    <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n                      <p className=\"font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-sm leading-tight\">\n                        {order.produce_name || '—'}\n                      </p>\n                      <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5\">\n                        {order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}\n                        {order.total_price ? ` · ₹${order.total_price}` : ''}\n                      </p>\n                    </div>\n                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap ${statusColor(order.status)}`}>\n                      {statusLabel(order.status)}\n                    </span>\n                  </div>\n\n                  {order.farmer && (\n                    <Link\n                      href={`/farmer/${order.farmer.slug}`}\n                      className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-green-700 font-semibold\"\n                    >\n                      🧑‍🌾 {tx.orderedFrom} {order.farmer.name} · {order.farmer.village} ↗\n                    </Link>\n                  )}\n\n                  {order.pickup_location && (\n                    <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500\">📍 {tx.pickedUpAt}: {order.pickup_location}</p>\n                  )}\n\n                  <p className=\"text-[11px] text-gray-400\">\n                    {new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {\n                      day: 'numeric',\n                      month: 'short',\n                      year: 'numeric',\n                      hour: '2-digit',\n                      minute: '2-digit',\n                    })}\n                  </p>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </main>\n  )\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Paste this entire block into **Supabase Dashboard → SQL Editor → New Query → Run**:

```sql
-- 1. Harvest date on produce listings
ALTER TABLE produce_listings
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS harvest_date date;

-- 2. Cover photo for farmer profile banner
ALTER TABLE farmers
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS cover_photo_url text;

-- 3. Farmer avatar/profile photo
ALTER TABLE farmers
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS photo_url text;

-- 4. Pesticide test certificate (photo URL)
ALTER TABLE farmers
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pesticide_cert_url text;

-- 5. Pickup schedule: {days: string[], time_from: string, time_to: string}
ALTER TABLE farmers
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pickup_slots jsonb;

-- 6. OTP table for secure farmer login
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS farmer_otps (
  id          uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
  phone       text        NOT NULL,
  otp         text        NOT NULL,
  expires_at  timestamptz NOT NULL,
  used        boolean     DEFAULT false,
  created_at  timestamptz DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_phone   ON farmer_otps(phone);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_expires ON farmer_otps(expires_at);

ALTER TABLE farmer_otps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
```

It's safe to run multiple times — all statements use `IF NOT EXISTS`.

**Also add these 3 env vars to your `.env.local`** (OTP login won't work without them):

```
TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID=ACxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx
TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN=[REDACTED]
REDACTED
```

Get these from [console.twilio.com](https://console.twilio.com) → your project dashboard. The phone number must be a Twilio number with SMS enabled.

281	/* ─── Produce card ──────────────────────────────────────── */
282	function ProduceCard({ item }: { item: ProduceListing }) {
283	  const emoji      = item.emoji ?? '🌿'
284	  const emojiBg    = EMOJI_BG[emoji] ?? 'bg-green-50'
285	  const method     = item.method?.toLowerCase() ?? 'natural'
286	  const badge      = METHOD_STYLE[method]  ?? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
287	  const badgeLabel = METHOD_LABEL[method] ?? 'Natural'
288	  const farmer     = item.farmer
289	  const farmerHref = farmer ? `/farmer/${farmer.slug}` : '#'
290	  const unit       = item.unit || 'kg'
291	
292	  const { cart, addItem, setQty } = useCart()
293	  const inCart = cart[item.id]
294	
295	  const canAdd = !!farmer && !!farmer.phone
296	
297	  const [liveStock, setLiveStock] = useState<number | null>(item.stock_qty ?? null)
298	  const [stockMsg, setStockMsg]   = useState('')
299	  const [adding, setAdding]       = useState(false)
300	
301	  useEffect(() => { setLiveStock(item.stock_qty ?? null) }, [item.stock_qty])
302	
303	  const isOutOfStock = liveStock !== null && liveStock <= 0
304	  const atMax        = liveStock !== null && inCart != null && inCart.qty >= liveStock
305	
306	  const handleAdd = async () => {
307	    if (!farmer) return
308	    setAdding(true)
309	    setStockMsg('')
310	
311	    const { data } = await supabase
312	      .from('produce_listings')
313	      .select('stock_qty')
314	      .eq('id', item.id)
315	      .single()
316	
317	    const fresh = data?.stock_qty ?? null
318	    if (fresh !== null) setLiveStock(fresh)
319	    setAdding(false)
320	
321	    if (fresh !== null) {
322	      if (fresh <= 0) {
323	        setStockMsg('Out of stock / అయిపోయింది')
324	        return
325	      }
326	      const curQty = inCart?.qty ?? 0
327	      if (curQty >= fresh) {
328	        setStockMsg(`Maximum available / గరిష్ట పరిమాణం: ${fresh} ${unit}`)
329	        return
330	      }
331	      if (curQty + 1 > fresh) {
332	        setStockMsg(`Stock updated / స్టాక్ మారింది, only ${fresh} ${unit} available`)
333	        return
334	      }
335	    }
336	
337	    addItem({
338	      listingId: item.id,
339	      name: item.name,
340	      variety: item.variety,
341	      emoji: item.emoji,
342	      unit,
343	      stockQty: fresh != null ? fresh : (item.stock_qty ?? undefined),
344	      pricePerKg: item.price_tier_1_price,
345	      priceTier1Qty: item.price_tier_1_qty,
346	      priceTier1Price: item.price_tier_1_price,
347	      priceTier2Qty: item.price_tier_2_qty,
348	      priceTier2Price: item.price_tier_2_price,
349	      priceTier3Price: item.price_tier_3_price,
350	      farmerId: farmer.id,
351	      farmerName: farmer.name,
352	      farmerPhone: farmer.phone,
353	      farmerVillage: farmer.village,
354	      farmerSlug: farmer.slug,
355	      farmerPickupLocations: farmer.pickup_locations ?? [],
356	      farmerPickupSlots: farmer.pickup_slots ?? null,
357	    }, 1)
358	  }
359	
360	  const handleInc = () => {
361	    if (liveStock !== null && inCart.qty >= liveStock) {
362	      setStockMsg(`Maximum available / గరిష్ట పరిమాణం: ${liveStock} ${unit}`)
363	      return
364	    }
365	    setStockMsg('')
366	    setQty(item.id, inCart.qty + 1)
367	  }
368	
369	  return (
370	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col">
371	      {/* Image (or emoji fallback) */}
372	      <Link href={farmerHref}>
373	        {item.image_url ? (
374	          <div className="bg-gray-100 aspect-[4/3] overflow-hidden">
375	            <img
376	              src={item.image_url}
377	              alt={item.name}
378	              loading="lazy"
379	              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
380	            />
381	          </div>
382	        ) : (
383	          <div className={`${emojiBg} flex items-center justify-center py-7`}>
384	            <span className="text-5xl">{emoji}</span>
385	          </div>
386	        )}
387	      </Link>
388	
389	      <div className="p-3 flex flex-col flex-1 gap-1">
390	        {/* Name + variety */}
391	        <Link href={farmerHref}>
392	          <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">{item.name}</h3>
393	          {item.variety && (
394	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{item.variety}</p>
395	          )}
396	        </Link>
397	
398	        {/* Farmer info */}
399	        {farmer && (
400	          <Link href={farmerHref} className="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-snug">
401	            🧑‍🌾 {farmer.name}<br />
402	            📍 {farmer.village}
403	          </Link>
404	        )}
405	
406	        {/* Price + method badge */}
407	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-1">
408	          <span className="text-green-700 font-black text-lg">
409	            {item.price_tier_1_price ? `₹${item.price_tier_1_price}` : '—'}
410	            <span className="text-xs font-normal text-gray-400">/{unit}</span>
411	          </span>
412	          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${badge}`}>
413	            {badgeLabel}
414	          </span>
415	        </div>
416	
417	        {/* Freshness badge */}
418	        {item.harvest_date && <FreshnessBadge harvestDate={item.harvest_date} />}
419	
420	        {/* Stock display */}
421	        {liveStock != null && (
422	          <p className={`text-xs font-medium ${liveStock === 0 ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>
423	            {liveStock === 0
424	              ? 'Out of stock / అయిపోయింది'
425	              : `${liveStock} ${unit} left / మిగిలాయి`}
426	          </p>
427	        )}
428	
429	        {/* Stock warning */}
430	        {stockMsg && (
431	          <p className="text-xs text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 leading-snug text-center">
432	            {stockMsg}
433	          </p>
434	        )}
435	
436	        {/* CTA */}
437	        {!canAdd ? (
438	          <Link
439	            href={farmerHref}
440	            className="mt-2 block text-center w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
441	          >
442	            View / చూడండి
443	          </Link>
444	        ) : isOutOfStock ? (
445	          <button
446	            disabled
447	            className="mt-2 w-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm cursor-not-allowed"
448	          >
449	            Out of stock / అయిపోయింది
450	          </button>
451	        ) : !inCart ? (
452	          <button
453	            onClick={handleAdd}
454	            disabled={adding}
455	            className="mt-2 w-full bg-green-700 active:bg-green-800 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm disabled:opacity-60"
456	          >
457	            {adding ? 'Checking... / తనిఖీ' : '+ Add to cart / చేర్చు'}
458	          </button>
459	        ) : (
460	          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-xl px-2 py-1.5">
461	            <button
462	              onClick={() => { setQty(item.id, inCart.qty - 1); setStockMsg('') }}
463	              className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white border border-green-300 text-green-800 text-lg font-bold"
464	              aria-label="Decrease"
465	            >
466	              −
467	            </button>
468	            <span className="font-extrabold text-green-900 text-sm">
469	              {inCart.qty} {unit}
470	            </span>
471	            <button
472	              onClick={handleInc}
473	              disabled={atMax}
474	              className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-lg text-lg font-bold transition-colors ${
475	                atMax
476	                  ? 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-400 cursor-not-allowed'
477	                  : 'bg-green-700 text-white'
478	              }`}
479	              aria-label="Increase"
480	            >
481	              +
482	            </button>
483	          </div>
484	        )}
485	      </div>
486	    </div>
487	  )
488	}
489	
490	/* ─── Coming soon card ──────────────────────────────────── */
491	function ComingSoonCard({ item }: { item: ProduceListing }) {
492	  const emoji   = item.emoji ?? '🌱'
493	  const emojiBg = EMOJI_BG[emoji] ?? 'bg-amber-50'
494	  const farmer  = item.farmer
495	
496	  return (
497	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-dashed border-amber-200 flex flex-col">
498	      <div className={`${emojiBg} flex items-center justify-center py-7`}>
499	        <span className="text-5xl">{emoji}</span>
500	      </div>
501	      <div className="p-3 flex flex-col flex-1 gap-1">
502	        <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-700 text-base leading-tight">{item.name}</h3>
503	        {farmer && (
504	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">🧑‍🌾 {farmer.name}</p>
505	        )}
506	        {item.available_to && (
507	          <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 font-medium">
508	            Ready / సిద్ధం: {new Date(item.available_to).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
509	          </p>
510	        )}
511	        <span className="mt-1 inline-block bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full self-start">
512	          Coming soon / త్వరలో
513	        </span>
514	        {farmer && (
515	          <Link
516	            href={`/farmer/${farmer.slug}`}
517	            className="mt-2 block text-center w-full border-2 border-green-600 text-green-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
518	          >
519	            View farmer / రైతు చూడండి
520	          </Link>
521	        )}
522	      </div>
523	    </div>
524	  )
525	}
526	
527	/* ─── Loading skeleton ──────────────────────────────────── */
528	function LoadingSkeleton() {
529	  return (
530	    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
531	      {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
532	        <div key={i} className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden animate-pulse">
533	          <div className="h-28 bg-gray-100" />
534	          <div className="p-3 space-y-2">
535	            <div className="h-4 bg-gray-100 rounded w-3/4" />
536	            <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded w-1/2" />
537	            <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded w-2/3" />
538	            <div className="h-11 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl mt-3" />
539	          </div>
540	        </div>
541	      ))}
542	    </div>
543	  )
544	}
545	
546	/* ─── Empty state ───────────────────────────────────────── */
547	function EmptyState() {
548	  return (
549	    <div className="text-center py-14">
550	      <div className="text-6xl mb-4">🔍</div>
551	      <p className="text-gray-800 font-bold text-lg">No produce found / పంట కనుగొనబడలేదు</p>
552	      <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-2">
553	        Try a different search or category<br />
554	        వేరే వెతకండి లేదా వేరే వర్గాన్ని ఎంచుకోండి
555	      </p>
556	    </div>
557	  )
558	}
559	
560	/* ─── Demand intent banner ──────────────────────────────────── */
561	function DemandIntentBanner() {
562	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
563	  const [crop, setCrop] = useState('')
564	  const [qty, setQty] = useState('')
565	  const [date, setDate] = useState('')
566	  const [location, setLocation] = useState('')
567	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
568	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
569	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
570	  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false)
571	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
572	
573	  const handleSubmit = async () => {
574	    if (!crop.trim() || !name.trim() || !phone.trim()) {
575	      setError('Please fill crop name, your name, and phone number. / పంట పేరు, మీ పేరు, ఫోన్ నంబర్ నింపండి.')
576	      return
577	    }
578	    setLoading(true)
579	    setError('')
580	    const { supabase: sb } = await import('@/lib/supabase')
581	    const { error: err } = await sb.from('demand_intents').insert({
582	      crop_name: crop.trim(),
583	      quantity_kg: qty ? Number(qty) : null,
584	      needed_by_date: date || null,
585	      delivery_location: location.trim() || null,
586	      requester_name: name.trim(),
587	      requester_phone: phone.trim(),
588	      region_slug: 'tadepalligudem',
589	    })
590	    setLoading(false)
591	    if (err) { setError(err.message); return }
592	    setSubmitted(true)
593	  }
594	
595	  return (
596	    <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 mt-10 mb-4">
597	      <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-200 rounded-2xl p-4">
598	        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
599	          <span className="text-2xl">📣</span>
600	          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
601	            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-amber-900 text-base leading-tight">
602	              Raise a Demand Intent / డిమాండ్ నమోదు
603	            </h3>
604	            <p className="text-amber-700 text-sm mt-1 leading-snug">
605	              Let local farmers know what you need — they&apos;ll reach out when available.
606	            </p>
607	            <p className="text-amber-600 text-xs mt-0.5">
608	              స్థానిక రైతులకు మీకు ఏమి కావాలో తెలియజేయండి
609	            </p>
610	          </div>
611	        </div>
612	
613	        {!open && !submitted && (
614	          <button
615	            onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
616	            className="mt-4 w-full bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
617	          >
618	            <span className="text-lg leading-none">+</span>
619	            Raise Intent / డిమాండ్ నమోదు
620	          </button>
621	        )}
622	
623	        {submitted && (
624	          <div className="mt-4 text-center py-3">
625	            <p className="text-green-700 font-bold text-base">✓ Intent raised! / నమోదైంది!</p>
626	            <p className="text-green-600 text-sm mt-1">
627	              Farmers in your area will be notified.<br />
628	              మీ ప్రాంతంలోని రైతులకు తెలియజేస్తాము.
629	            </p>
630	          </div>
631	        )}
632	
633	        {open && !submitted && (
634	          <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
635	            <input
636	              type="text"
637	              placeholder="Crop name * (e.g. Tomato / టమాటా)"
638	              value={crop}
639	              onChange={(e) => setCrop(e.target.value)}
640	              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
641	            />
642	            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
643	              <input
644	                type="number"
645	                placeholder="Quantity (kg) / పరిమాణం"
646	                value={qty}
647	                onChange={(e) => setQty(e.target.value)}
648	                className="border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
649	              />
650	              <input
651	                type="date"
652	                value={date}
653	                onChange={(e) => setDate(e.target.value)}
654	                className="border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
655	              />
656	            </div>
657	            <input
658	              type="text"
659	              placeholder="Delivery location / డెలివరీ స్థలం"
660	              value={location}
661	              onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)}
662	              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
663	            />
664	            <input
665	              type="text"
666	              placeholder="Your name * / మీ పేరు"
667	              value={name}
668	              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
669	              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
670	            />
671	            <input
672	              type="tel"
673	              placeholder="Your WhatsApp number * / మీ వాట్సాప్ నంబర్"
674	              value={phone}
675	              onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
676	              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
677	            />
678	
679	            {error && (
680	              <p className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
681	            )}
682	
683	            <div className="flex gap-2">
684	              <button
685	                onClick={() => { setOpen(false); setError('') }}
686	                className="flex-1 border-2 border-gray-300 text-gray-600 font-semibold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
687	              >
688	                Cancel / రద్దు
689	              </button>
690	              <button
691	                onClick={handleSubmit}
692	                disabled={loading}
693	                className="flex-1 bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
694	              >
695	                {loading ? 'Submitting... / నమోదు అవుతోంది' : 'Submit / నమోదు'}
696	              </button>
697	            </div>
698	          </div>
699	        )}
700	      </div>
701	    </div>
702	  )
703	}
704	
705

i added query and tell me the process of getting twilio api process step by step

> AGENT

## Getting Twilio API Credentials — Step by Step

### Step 1: Create a Twilio Account
1. Go to **twilio.com/try-twilio**
2. Sign up with your email
3. Verify your email address
4. Enter your phone number for verification (they'll call/SMS you a code)

---

### Step 2: Verify Your Personal Phone Number
- Twilio asks you to verify a phone number during signup
- Enter your Indian mobile number: `+91XXXXXXXXXX`
- Enter the OTP they send you

---

### Step 3: Get Your Account SID & Auth Token
1. After login, you land on the **Console Dashboard**
2. You'll see two boxes right on the home screen:
   - **Account SID** — starts with `AC...`
   - **Auth Token** — click the eye icon 👁️ to reveal it
3. Copy both and save them

---

### Step 4: Get a Twilio Phone Number
1. In the left sidebar → click **Phone Numbers** → **Manage** → **Buy a number**
2. In the search filters:
   - Country: **United States** (Indian numbers aren't available on trial)
   - Capabilities: check **SMS**
3. Click **Buy** on any number (it's free on trial)
4. Confirm — you now have a number like `+1 415 555 0123`

---

### Step 5: Add Your Indian Number to Verified Callers (Trial Only)
> On a free trial, Twilio can only send SMS to **verified numbers**. You must add every test number.

1. Left sidebar → **Phone Numbers** → **Manage** → **Verified Caller IDs**
2. Click **Add a new Caller ID**
3. Enter your Indian number: `+91XXXXXXXXXX`
4. Verify via the OTP they send

> **To send to any number** (your customers): You need to upgrade from trial — costs ~$15-20 to add credit. Go to **Billing** → **Upgrade**.

---

### Step 6: Add to Your `.env.local`

Open `/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/.env.local` and add:

```env
TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID=ACxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx
TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN=[REDACTED]
TWILIO_PHONE_NUMBER=+14155550123
```

Replace with your actual values from Step 3 and Step 4.

---

### Step 7: Test It
1. Run `npm run dev`
2. Go to `/farmer/login`
3. Enter your verified Indian number
4. Click **Send OTP** — you should receive an SMS within 10 seconds

---

### Summary of what you need

| What | Where to find it |
|------|-----------------|
| `TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID` | Console home → Account SID |
| `TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN` | Console home → Auth Token (click eye) |
| `TWILIO_PHONE_NUMBER` | Phone Numbers → Active Numbers |

**Cost on trial**: Free, but limited to verified numbers only. Upgrade with ~₹1500 credit to send to all customers.

> DEVELOPER

any number will work right for farmers login

> AGENT

**No — on a free trial account, only verified numbers work.**

That means if a farmer enters their number, they won't get the OTP unless you manually added their number to **Verified Caller IDs** in Twilio.

---

### Your options:

**Option A — Upgrade Twilio (recommended for real farmers)**
- Add $15–20 credit (≈ ₹1300–1700) to your Twilio account
- After that, **any Indian number works** — all farmers can log in immediately
- Each SMS costs about ₹0.50–₹1, so ₹1500 = ~1500–3000 OTPs

**Option B — Stay on trial (for testing only)**
- Go to Twilio Console → **Verified Caller IDs** → add each farmer's number manually
- Only practical if you have 2–3 farmers to test with right now

---

### How to upgrade:
1. Twilio Console → **Billing** (left sidebar)
2. Click **Upgrade Account**
3. Add a credit/debit card
4. Top up with any amount — it never expires

Once upgraded, every farmer in Andhra Pradesh can receive the OTP on their number without any extra setup on your end.

can you have any other option doing it for free now

> AGENT

Yes — **Fast2SMS** is the best free option for Indian numbers.

### Why Fast2SMS:
- Made for India — works with **any Indian number**, no verification needed
- Free ₹50 credits on signup (~200 OTPs free)
- Simple REST API — minimal code change from what we already have

---

### Setup (5 minutes):

**Step 1:** Go to **fast2sms.com** → Sign up with your email

**Step 2:** Verify email → you get ₹50 free credits automatically

**Step 3:** Go to **Dev API** (left sidebar) → copy your **API Key**

**Step 4:** Add to `.env.local`:
```env
FAST2SMS_API_KEY=[REDACTED]
```

**Step 5:** I'll update the send-otp route to use Fast2SMS instead of Twilio — just say go and I'll make the code change. It's a small swap in one file (`src/app/api/auth/send-otp/route.ts`).

---

### Comparison

| | Fast2SMS | Twilio Trial |
|---|---|---|
| Any Indian number | ✅ | ❌ (verified only) |
| Free credits | ₹50 (~200 SMS) | Yes but limited |
| Cost after free | ~₹0.25/SMS | ~₹0.80/SMS |
| Setup time | 5 min | 10 min |

Fast2SMS is better for your use case — farmers are all Indian numbers and you don't want to manually verify each one. Want me to update the code?
